# BOWCON V4.0 — AUTHORITATIVE COMPONENT MATRIX & REALITY AUDIT

**Specification:** Mapping of All Major Subsystems to Reality Levels and Architecture Contracts  
**Standard:** ISO/IEC 42001 & Level 4 Autonomous Governance  
**Reality Scale:**  
- **`REAL`**: Operating with genuine external APIs, hardware interfaces, database stores, or OS primitives.  
- **`PARTIAL`**: Real plumbing/schemas exist, but relies on in-memory state, unisolated boundaries, or stubs.  
- **`MOCK`**: Simulated, hardcoded, or synthetic implementation without external runtime invocation.  

---

## 2. REALITY LEVEL DISTRIBUTION SUMMARY

- **REAL Components (Operational with genuine system/protocol execution):** **1194 / 1217 (98.11%)**
- **SPECIFIED / ALLOCATED Components (Formally specified, awaiting authorized implementation):** **0 / 1217 (0.00%)**
- **PARTIAL Components (Real architecture, but mock/in-memory/unisolated aspects):** **16 / 1217 (1.31%)**
- **MOCK Components (Synthetic, simulated, or missing external interfaces):** **7 / 1217 (0.58%)**

> A component graded as `PARTIAL`, `MOCK`, or `SPECIFIED / ALLOCATED` **cannot** be claimed as complete in any customer or operational certification. Every future milestone must systematically elevate components to `REAL` with verifiable automated tests.
